# Requirement Closure Matrix

## Purpose

Every clause of `FEATURES.md` (SSO-1.1 to SSO-12.6, 101 clauses), split into obligations that can each be proven on
their own: the behaviour, its negative cases, its defaults, its transaction guarantees and what the documentation must
say. Each row names the code that implements the obligation, the test that proves it and the assertion that does, the
documentation that describes it and, where it falls short, the gap and the item that closes it (BL-84).

Reviewed tree: commit `cd17e39cc9612e5b489261340a6c156367607765` on branch `feat/p8-closure` (P8, the closure of the
P0 to P7 gaps, BL-80 to BL-83 and their integration, on `master` at `43b8240d345f8cb50d7b1d0ddc050f003aadaa86` with P0
to P7 merged), with the owner's P8 decisions, the P8 recommended defaults and the guides' examples taken from executed
sources applied on top, as it stood on 2026-11-03. Every row was checked by reading the implementing code and the body
of the cited test, never by a name alone. An independent audit then read every row again; the rows it found wrong are
corrected here, with the missing proof written and the missing rows added. After P8's items and their integration
landed, every row was checked again against the final tree: every cited file, test method and symbol exists, every
quoted passage is where the row says, every link resolves, and the rows the items and the integration touched were
read again. The owner's P8 decisions and the recommended defaults taken with them unless the owner objects
(2026-11-03) then settled the nine rows that waited on a meaning, with the code, tests and documentation following
them, and those rows and every row a change touched were read again; on the final tree every row was checked once more
for its cited files, test methods, links, decisions and counts. Sibling names marked PR #127 are
`laravel-authentication`'s phase 14a final names, merged to its `master` as `c7b6b119` on 2026-11-03; no tagged
release carries them yet.

Clauses are cited by their `FEATURES.md` id. Recorded decisions are cited by their entry in [Recorded
decisions](#recorded-decisions), which restates each one with the section of the package's backlog it was recorded in
and its date, so a reader of this matrix alone can read what every reconciled row relies on.

## Reading the Matrix

- **Id** is the clause and a letter for each obligation (`SSO-6.3/b`). `SSO-6/a`, `SSO-7/a` and `SSO-9/a` to `SSO-9/c`
  are the obligations of the preambles of sections 6, 7 and 9; the `CTX/...` rows are the context section's, outside the
  101 clauses.
- **Status** is one of `covered` (implemented, and a test proves it), `reconciled_by_decision` (a later recorded
  decision changed what the clause asks; the row cites it, and the code and tests follow the decision),
  `missing_implementation` (the code or the documentation does not exist), `missing_proof` (the behaviour exists but no
  test would fail without it), `contradictory_docs` (a document or docblock says what the code or a decision does not)
  and `unresolved_meaning` (the clause can be read two ways and no decision settles it).
- **Severity** applies to a gap only: `critical`, `high`, `medium` or `low`.
- **Closure** names the item that closes a gap: BL-80 (testing helpers guide), BL-81 (authentication context
  contributor and session provenance), BL-82 (seam documentation for `laravel-iam`), BL-83 (documentation and README),
  or GAP for P0 to P7 behaviour and proof.
- **Test** cites the authoritative file and method, with the dataset rows in brackets where they carry the proof. A
  row that says "see" is proven by the rows it names. A documentation obligation has no test: its assertion quotes or
  describes what the document says.
- **Documentation** links the heading that describes the obligation.

| SSO-2.8/q | For OIDC too, a failed refresh keeps the last good settings and keys, and the stale limit expires a JWKS from its own validated fetch                          | `Metadata\OidcMetadataSource`, `Metadata\MetadataExpiry`                                                    | `tests/Feature/Metadata/OidcMetadataSourceTest.php::testAFailureKeepsTheLastGoodSettingsAndKeys` [each failure]; `tests/Feature/Metadata/MetadataExpiryTest.php::testTheJwksExpiresFromItsOwnFetch`                                                                                                                | settings and keys unchanged after each failure, with its reason; the JWKS expires at its own fetch plus the stale limit                                           | [docs/guides/oidc-connections.md#the-30-day-stale-limit](../guides/oidc-connections.md#the-30-day-stale-limit)           | covered                | -        | none    | -                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| SSO-2.8/r | A SAML connection not opted in to refresh behaves like hand-entered certificates: no automatic change and no stale deadline                                    | `Metadata\MetadataExpiry`, `Connections\ImportSaver`                                                        | `tests/Feature/Metadata/MetadataExpiryTest.php::testASamlDocumentHoldsTheDeadlineItsRefreshGivesIt`; `tests/Feature/Connections/ImportSaverTest.php::testASamlImportStartsAClockOnlyWhereItHoldsADeadline` [each case]                                                                                             | a SAML document holds only the deadline its refresh gives it; an import starts a clock only where the connection refreshes                                        | [docs/guides/saml-metadata.md#refreshing-on-a-schedule](../guides/saml-metadata.md#refreshing-on-a-schedule)             | reconciled_by_decision | -        | none    | [RD-13](#recorded-decisions): not opted in behaves like hand-entered certificates, with no automatic change and no stale deadline                                                                                                                                          |

the entity id, subject source and attribute and NameID format are the subject settings                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | [docs/guides/managing-connections.md#settings-that-change-what-subjects-mean](../guides/managing-connections.md#settings-that-change-what-subjects-mean) | covered                | -        | none    | -                                                                                                           |
| SSO-2.13/c | An explicit migration helper relinks or removes every link, accounting for each exactly once                                                 | `Connections\SubjectMigration`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `tests/Feature/Connections/SubjectMigrationTest.php::testRelinksAndRemovesTheLinks`, `::testRefusesADispositionThatDoesNotAccountForEveryLink`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `assertSame(ConnectionStatus::MIGRATED, $result->status)`, links replaced, `LinkCreated` with `LinkOrigin::MIGRATION`; an unaccounted link is refused                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | [docs/guides/managing-connections.md#migrating-subjects](../guides/managing-connections.md#migrating-subjects)                                           | covered                | -        | none    | -                                                                                                           |
| SSO-2.13/d | Any trust change voids the connection's in-flight authorisation requests and unredeemed handoff codes                                        | `Connections\InFlightVoider` (voids, and raises `trust_version`, a disable and the enable after it once each); `Completion\Stages\IssueHandoff` (the grant's `trustVersion`, read from the locked connection in T2 or T3); `Handoffs\DatabaseHandoffStore` (`trust_version` kept with the code); `Handoffs\HandoffRecheck` (`admit()` and `recheck()`, the package's `redeem()` and `admit()` included, refuse a binding whose `trustVersion` is not the locked connection's, or names none); `Contracts\HandoffIssuer` (requires an application's store to keep and answer it) | `tests/Feature/Connections/TrustChangeVoidingTest.php::testATrustChangeVoidsOnlyWhatIsInFlight`, `::testEachTrustChangeVoids`; `tests/Integration/Handoffs/HandoffTrustVersionTest.php::testATrustChangeBetweenIssuanceAndRedemptionRefusesTheCode` [the certificates replaced, a certificate distrusted, an anchor trusted, disabled then enabled], `::testADisableAndTheEnableAfterItEachRaiseTheTrustVersion`, `::testAVersionRaisedWithoutTheVoidIsStillRefused`; `tests/Integration/Examples/RedeemingAHandoff/OwnHandoffCodesExampleTest.php::testATrustChangeRefusesACodeIssuedBeforeIt` [the same four], `::testABindingNamingNoTrustVersionIsRefused`; `tests/Feature/Handoffs/HandoffRecheckTest.php::testABindingOfAnotherTrustVersionIsConnectionUnavailable`; `tests/Integration/Handoffs/TrustChangeRedemptionRaceTest.php::testARedemptionWaitingOnATrustChangeIsRefused`, `::testARedemptionWaitingOnATrustChangeInsideCallersTransactionsIsRefused`, `::testATrustChangeWaitingOnARedemptionLetsItBeAdmitted` [PostgreSQL, MySQL] | pending request and unredeemed code stamped voided; finished request and redeemed code untouched; the package's code issued before each change is `handoff_unknown`, the version raised by one (two for a disable and an enable), and the connection signs in afresh; with the version raised and the code left unvoided it is still `connection_unavailable`; an application's code is `connection_unavailable` after each change, and a binding naming no version is `connection_unavailable` where the version kept passes; the race, ordered by signals, refuses the redemption that waited on the change and admits the one the change waited on | [docs/design/connection-lifecycle.md#which-changes-void](connection-lifecycle.md#which-changes-void)                                                     | covered                | -        | none    | -                                                                                                           |
| SSO-2.13/e | An additive change of verification trust (a certificate or JWKS key added) voids nothing and keeps the trust version                         | `Keys\KeyService::replaceTrustedCertificatesLocked()`, `Metadata\MetadataApplier`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `tests/Feature/Keys/KeyServiceTest.php::testAnAdditiveReplacementVoidsNothing`; `tests/Feature/Metadata/MetadataApplierTest.php::testAnAdditiveCertificateVoidsNothing`; `tests/Integration/Handoffs/HandoffTrustVersionTest.php::testAnAddedCertificateRefusesNothing`; `tests/Integration/Examples/RedeemingAHandoff/OwnHandoffCodesExampleTest.php::testAnAddedCertificateRefusesNothing`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | the trust version is kept and the flow in flight is not voided; a code issued before a certificate added alone, of the package's store or an application's, is admitted                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | [docs/design/connection-lifecycle.md#which-changes-void](connection-lifecycle.md#which-changes-void)                                                     | reconciled_by_decision | -        | none    | FEATURES: 'any trust change voids'; [RD-07](#recorded-decisions): additive verification keys do not bump it |

### SSO-4.2 Which tenant's rule applies

| SSO-6/a | Every check of section 6 is always on for a SAML connection, whatever its settings: no connection setting or global value turns one off | `Protocols\Saml2\Enums\Saml2Setting` (every setting), `Saml2Settings` validation, `Saml2Limits` (bounded limits), `Config\Features` | `tests/Feature/Protocols/SettingsInventoryTest.php::testEverySamlSettingIsAccountedFor`, `::testAWeakeningValueIsRefused` [a transient name id, a sha-1 signature, no signature algorithm, rsa 1.5 key transport], `::testEveryChoosingSettingRefusesItsWeakeningValueOrIsProvenElsewhere`; `tests/Feature/Completion/SamlAdversarialCompletionTest.php::testAnUnsignedAssertionPassesOnlyThroughTheResponsesSignature`; `tests/Feature/Protocols/Saml2/Saml2LimitsTest.php::testEachLimitIsHeldToItsBounds` [each limit] | every SAML setting is placed as `identifies`, `requests`, `tightens` or `chooses`, and a new one fails until placed; each value that would weaken a check is a violation; every `chooses` setting is refused there or names the test proving its floor (`want_assertions_signed`, `idp_initiated`, `metadata_refresh`), and a new one with neither fails; `want_assertions_signed` off still needs the response's trusted signature; the issue-instant age and size caps refuse a value outside their bounds | [docs/design/saml-responses.md#invariants](saml-responses.md#invariants) | covered | -        | none    | -     |

### SSO-11.1 Stateless requests

## Recorded Decisions

Every decision a row cites, restated so this matrix can be read alone. Each was recorded in the package's backlog
under the section named, on the date given; the P3 to P7 entries and those marked "P8 design agreed" were reviewed
with the owner. Those marked "P8 decisions" (RD-27 to RD-29) are the owner's own decisions of 2026-11-03. Those marked
"P8 recommended defaults" (RD-30 to RD-35) are not: they are the defaults recommended alongside those decisions, taken
unless the owner objects in the pull request that carries P8, and the code, tests and documentation follow them until
then.

| Id    | Recorded in                        | Date                   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                  | Rows                              |
|-------|------------------------------------|------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------|
| RD-01 | Decisions, D1                      | 2026-11-01             | SAML is built on `simplesamlphp/saml2` v6 with `xml-security`, advertising AES-128/192-GCM until v7, and moves to v7 when the PHP floor reaches 8.5.                                      | SSO-6.7/d                         |
| RD-02 | Sibling names                      | 2026-11-01             | Sibling names are for adapters and documentation only: nothing the package ships names a sibling or cites its requirements.                                                               | SSO-9/b                           |
| RD-03 | P3 design review                   | 2026-11-01             | Disabling a connection is a freshness operation of its own, `sso.connection.disable`.                                                                                                     | SSO-2.12/c                        |
| RD-04 | P3 design review                   | 2026-11-01             | Discovery's minimum duration is a mitigation of timing differences, not a guarantee that a hit and a miss are indistinguishable.                                                          | SSO-3.5/c                         |
| RD-05 | P3 design review (#8)              | 2026-11-01             | Disabling or deleting a rule's last satisfying connection is allowed: the rule stays on and the connection's sessions are revoked.                                                        | SSO-4.3/c                         |
| RD-06 | P4/P5 design agreed                | 2026-11-01             | Fetched metadata goes stale 30 days after it last validated, by default; only an explicit 0 removes the limit.                                                                            | SSO-2.8/n                         |
| RD-07 | P4/P5 design agreed                | 2026-11-01             | Adding a verification key does not raise a connection's trust version; every other trust change does.                                                                                     | SSO-2.13/e                        |
| RD-08 | P4/P5 design agreed                | 2026-11-01             | The OIDC token exchange is attempted once, with no transport or middleware retries; a timeout or a 5xx refuses and the request stays consumed.                                            | SSO-7.2/e                         |
| RD-09 | P4/P5 design agreed                | 2026-11-01             | UserInfo is off by default and, when on, mandatory and fail closed.                                                                                                                       | SSO-7.5/d                         |
| RD-10 | P4 design review                   | 2026-11-01             | SAML stays on PHP 8.3 and saml2 v6 with RSA signatures only, ECDSA and AES-256-GCM waiting for an 8.5 floor and a stable v7.                                                              | SSO-6.7/b, SSO-6.7/d              |
| RD-11 | P4 design review                   | 2026-11-01             | A SAML response's `Destination` is required and must equal the assertion consumer service exactly, signed or not.                                                                         | SSO-6.4/f                         |
| RD-12 | P5 design review                   | 2026-11-02             | Scheduled metadata refresh is on by default (OIDC, and SAML with a metadata URL), with the global feature default aligned.                                                                | SSO-2.8/o, SSO-12.2/b, CTX/b      |
| RD-13 | P5 design review                   | 2026-11-02             | SAML refresh without a signing anchor is opt-in only; an unanchored connection not opted in behaves like hand-entered certificates, with no automatic change and no stale deadline.       | SSO-2.8/h, SSO-2.8/r, CTX/b       |
| RD-14 | P5 design review                   | 2026-11-02             | A non-boolean `email_verified` is unknown: only JSON `true` is evidence, and nothing is coerced.                                                                                          | SSO-7.7/b                         |
| RD-15 | P6 design agreed                   | 2026-11-02             | Provisioning runs outside the package's locks, so a provisioned identity can survive a later completion failure.                                                                          | SSO-8.6/j                         |
| RD-16 | P6 design agreed                   | 2026-11-02             | A change to linking settings alone raises a separate `linking_version`, never the protocol trust version.                                                                                 | SSO-8.8/p                         |
| RD-17 | P6 design agreed                   | 2026-11-02             | Re-authentication accepts only the exact link that established the session.                                                                                                               | SSO-9.5/c                         |
| RD-18 | P6 build review                    | 2026-11-02             | A link's `last_used_at` is touched by a `sign_in` only.                                                                                                                                   | SSO-8.4/c                         |
| RD-19 | P6 build review                    | 2026-11-02             | Confirming a verified domain link needs independent proof, across connections, through `LinkConfirmation`, whose default refuses.                                                         | SSO-8.5/d                         |
| RD-20 | P6 build review                    | 2026-11-02             | Sign-in codes redeem only through the transactional API; the standalone `redeem()` keeps `link` codes alone.                                                                              | SSO-9.9/b, SSO-12.1/c, SSO-12.5/f |
| RD-21 | P7 design agreed                   | 2026-11-02             | Protocol drivers are rebuilt once per request or job scope.                                                                                                                               | SSO-11.2/b                        |
| RD-22 | P7 design agreed                   | 2026-11-02             | The schedule's dependence on the cache is documented, not worked around; scheduling is database-safe by default, with cache mutexes an explicit opt-in.                                   | SSO-11.6/b                        |
| RD-23 | P7 design agreed                   | 2026-11-02             | Requests and handoffs share one configurable expiry-based retention (one day by default), other kinds are removed at expiry, and an incomplete prune exits 0 with a warning.              | SSO-11.11/b                       |
| RD-24 | P7 design agreed                   | 2026-11-02             | Expiry warnings are at least once: a duplicate is possible, a silent loss is not.                                                                                                         | SSO-11.12/c                       |
| RD-25 | P7 design agreed; P8 design agreed | 2026-11-02, 2026-11-03 | SSO issues, stores and redeems every handoff code; authentication stores none and exposes only the external sign-in and re-authentication entry points the adapter calls after `admit()`. | SSO-9.2/f, SSO-9.9/b, SSO-12.5/f  |
| RD-26 | P8 design agreed (position 4)      | 2026-11-03             | A re-authentication is renewed once, inside `admit()`, and an adapter never renews it a second time.                                                                                      | SSO-12.5/f                        |
| RD-27 | P8 decisions                       | 2026-11-03             | The event context's `tenant_id` is the active tenant, as across the suite, and the connection's tenant is `connection_tenant_id`.                                                         | SSO-9.6/a, CTX/i                  |
| RD-28 | P8 decisions                       | 2026-11-03             | A connection's mapping names only the custom keys the application declares in `sso.mapping.custom_attributes`, which is empty by default.                                                 | SSO-2.10/a                        |
| RD-29 | P8 decisions                       | 2026-11-03             | Turning enforcement off revokes nothing, since no session falls out of compliance.                                                                                                        | SSO-4.5/i                         |
| RD-30 | P8 recommended defaults            | 2026-11-03             | A SAML response's `Issuer` is always required, successful or not, though SAML 2.0 Profiles lets an unsigned response with a plain assertion leave it out.                                 | SSO-6.4/a, SSO-6.11/c             |
| RD-31 | P8 recommended defaults            | 2026-11-03             | The exemption contract is asked only through the adapter's gate, `Enforcement\ExemptionGate`, never by the package's own completion or redemption.                                        | SSO-4.4/c                         |
| RD-32 | P8 recommended defaults            | 2026-11-03             | An OIDC metadata refresh may tighten `returns_iss` from false to true (never back), voiding what is in flight as a trust change.                                                          | SSO-2.8/p                         |
| RD-33 | P8 recommended defaults            | 2026-11-03             | Without discovery there is no fallback answer: an unknown or disabled connection is refused as `connection_unavailable`.                                                                  | SSO-3.5/d                         |
| RD-34 | P8 recommended defaults            | 2026-11-03             | The session keeps the enforcement version the redemption read under its lock, not the one the completion checked.                                                                         | SSO-9.10/a                        |
| RD-35 | P8 recommended defaults            | 2026-11-03             | Nothing being shaped to one consumer is a release review item and a change trigger, not a test.                                                                                           | CTX/e                             |

## Change Triggers

- A clause of `FEATURES.md` or a recorded decision changes: its rows are split or read again, and reconciliations
  cited again.
- An item of P8 lands: its rows are verified again against the new tree, and the reviewed commit above is updated.
- A cited test is renamed, moved or rewritten: its row is verified again against the new body.
- A cited implementation symbol is renamed, moved or rewritten: its row is verified again against the new code.
- A sibling releases the names marked PR #127, or changes them: every row citing one is verified again against the
  release, and Purpose is updated.
- A release is prepared (CTX/e, RD-35): `src/`, `config/`, `lang/` and the presets are reviewed for a name, a default
  or a branch only one consumer needs; a change to `Protocols\Presets`, `Linking\LinkingSettings` or
  `Contracts\EnforcementInheritance` triggers the same review.
- A recorded decision is added or changed: it is restated in Recorded decisions and every row citing it is read
  again.
